===== M0001 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 223-245
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
223 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
224 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
225 | 
226 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
227 |       // which are var_args.
228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
229 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
230 |            arg != null;
231 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
232 |         String argName = arg.getString();
233 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
234 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
235 | 
236 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
237 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
238 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
239 |         }
240 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
241 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
242 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
243 |         }
244 |       }
245 |     }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.getDirectedSuccNodes(DiGraphNode<N, E>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 240-251
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   @Override
241 |   public List<DiGraphNode<N, E>> getDirectedSuccNodes(
242 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode) {
243 |     if (dNode == null) {
244 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(dNode + " is null");
245 |     }
246 |     List<DiGraphNode<N, E>> nodeList = Lists.newArrayList();
247 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : dNode.getOutEdges()) {
248 |       nodeList.add(edge.getDestination());
249 |     }
250 |     return nodeList;
251 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineFunctionLiteral(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 756-776
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a function literal.
     */
Method code:
756 |     void defineFunctionLiteral(Node n, Node parent) {
757 |       assertDefinitionNode(n, Token.FUNCTION);
758 | 
759 |       // Determine the name and JSDocInfo and l-value for the function.
760 |       // Any of these may be null.
761 |       Node lValue = NodeUtil.getBestLValue(n);
762 |       JSDocInfo info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(n);
763 |       String functionName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(lValue);
764 |       FunctionType functionType =
765 |           createFunctionTypeFromNodes(n, functionName, info, lValue);
766 | 
767 |       // Assigning the function type to the function node
768 |       setDeferredType(n, functionType);
769 | 
770 |       // Declare this symbol in the current scope iff it's a function
771 |       // declaration. Otherwise, the declaration will happen in other
772 |       // code paths.
773 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
774 |         defineSlot(n.getFirstChild(), n, functionType);
775 |       }
776 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.ClosureReverseAbstractInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node,FlowScope,boolean)

Method code:
205 |   @Override
206 |   public FlowScope getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node condition,
207 |       FlowScope blindScope, boolean outcome) {
208 |     if (condition.isCall() && condition.getChildCount() == 2) {
209 |       Node callee = condition.getFirstChild();
210 |       Node param = condition.getLastChild();
211 |       if (callee.isGetProp() && param.isQualifiedName()) {
212 |         JSType paramType =  getTypeIfRefinable(param, blindScope);
213 |         Node left = callee.getFirstChild();
214 |         Node right = callee.getLastChild();
215 |         if (left.isName() && "goog".equals(left.getString()) &&
216 |             right.isString()) {
217 |           Function<TypeRestriction, JSType> restricter =
218 |               restricters.get(right.getString());
219 |           if (restricter != null) {
220 |             return restrictParameter(param, paramType, blindScope, restricter,
221 |                 outcome);
222 |           }
223 |         }
224 |       }
225 |     }
226 |     return nextPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
227 |         condition, blindScope, outcome);

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2820-2835
Documentation:
/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2820 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2821 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
2822 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2823 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2824 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2825 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2826 |       if (parent.isAssign()) {
2827 |         // on ASSIGNs
2828 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2829 |       } else if (parent.isName()) {
2830 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2831 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2832 |       }
2833 |     }
2834 |     return fnInfo;
2835 |   }

/**
   * Declares a variable.
   *
   * @param name name of the variable
   * @param nameNode the NAME node declaring the variable
   * @param type the variable's type
   * @param input the input in which this variable is defined.
   * @param inferred Whether this variable's type is inferred (as opposed
   *     to declared).

Method code:
498 |   Var declare(String name, Node nameNode,
499 |       JSType type, CompilerInput input, boolean inferred) {
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(name != null && name.length() > 0);
501 | 
502 |     // Make sure that it's declared only once

510 |         info != null && info.isDefine(), info);
511 | 
512 |     vars.put(name, var);
513 |     return var;
514 |   }

===== M0031 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 576-638
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
576 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
577 |     if (returnType == null) {
578 |       // Infer return types.
579 |       // We need to be extremely conservative about this, because of two
580 |       // competing needs.
581 |       // 1) If we infer the return type of f too widely, then we won't be able
582 |       //    to assign f to other functions.
583 |       // 2) If we infer the return type of f too narrowly, then we won't be
584 |       //    able to override f in subclasses.
585 |       // So we only infer in cases where the user doesn't expect to write
586 |       // @return annotations--when it's very obvious that the function returns
587 |       // nothing.
588 |       if (!contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() &&
589 |           !contents.mayHaveSingleThrow() &&
590 |           !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
591 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
592 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
593 |       }
594 |     }
595 | 
596 |     if (returnType == null) {
597 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
598 |     }
599 | 
600 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
601 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
602 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
603 |     }
604 | 
605 |     FunctionType fnType;
606 |     if (isConstructor) {
607 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
608 |     } else if (isInterface) {
609 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
610 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode());
611 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
612 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
613 |       }
614 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
615 |     } else {
616 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
617 |           .withName(fnName)
618 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
619 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
620 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
621 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
622 |           .withTemplateNames(templateTypeNames)
623 |           .build();
624 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
625 |     }
626 | 
627 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
628 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
632 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
633 |     }
634 | 
635 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeNames();
636 | 
637 |     return fnType;
638 |   }

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 466-496
Documentation:

Method code:
466 |     @Override
467 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
468 |         Node parent) {
469 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
470 |       if (n.isFunction() ||
471 |           n.isScript()) {
472 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
473 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
474 |       }
475 | 
476 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
477 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
478 |       boolean descend = parent == null || !parent.isFunction() ||
479 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
480 | 
481 |       if (descend) {
482 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
483 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
484 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
485 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
486 |                child != null;
487 |                child = child.getNext()) {
488 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
489 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child, n);
490 |             }
491 |           }
492 |         }
493 |       }
494 | 
495 |       return descend;
496 |     }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1267-1315
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1267 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1268 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1269 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1270 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1271 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1272 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1273 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1274 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1275 |       } else if (info != null) {
1276 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1277 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1278 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1279 |           } else {
1280 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1281 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1282 |           }
1283 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1284 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1285 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1286 |         } else {
1287 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1288 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1289 |             JSType knownType = null;
1290 |             if (rValue != null) {
1291 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1292 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1293 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1294 |               } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1295 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1296 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1297 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1298 |                 // reasons.
1299 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1300 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1301 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1302 |                     && lValue.isName()
1303 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1304 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1305 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1306 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1307 |                 }
1308 |               }
1309 |             }
1310 |           }
1311 |         }
1312 |       }
1313 | 
1314 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1315 |     }

===== M0059 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.getTypeIfRefinable(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 118-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns the type of a node in the given scope if the node corresponds to a
   * name whose type is capable of being refined.
   * @return The current type of the node if it can be refined, null otherwise.
   */
Method code:
118 |   protected JSType getTypeIfRefinable(Node node, FlowScope scope) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.NAME:
121 |         StaticSlot<JSType> nameVar = scope.getSlot(node.getString());
122 |         if (nameVar != null) {
123 |           JSType nameVarType = nameVar.getType();
124 |           if (nameVarType == null) {
125 |             nameVarType = node.getJSType();
126 |           }
127 |           return nameVarType;
128 |         }
129 |         return null;
130 | 
131 |       case Token.GETPROP:
132 |         String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
133 |         if (qualifiedName == null) {
134 |           return null;
135 |         }
136 |         StaticSlot<JSType> propVar = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
137 |         JSType propVarType = null;
138 |         if (propVar != null) {
139 |           propVarType = propVar.getType();
140 |         }
141 |         if (propVarType == null) {
142 |           propVarType = node.getJSType();
143 |         }
144 |         if (propVarType == null) {
145 |           propVarType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
146 |         }
147 |         return propVarType;
148 |     }
149 |     return null;
150 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 |   }

   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.

===== M0104 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3011-3037
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3011 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3012 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3013 |     if (info == null) {
3014 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3015 |       if (parent == null) {
3016 |         return null;
3017 |       }
3018 | 
3019 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3020 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3021 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3022 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3023 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3024 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3025 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3026 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3027 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3028 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3029 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3030 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3031 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3032 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3033 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3034 |       }
3035 |     }
3036 |     return info;
3037 |   }

===== M0110 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 421-489
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
421 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
422 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
423 |     if (argsParent == null) {
424 |       if (info == null) {
425 |         return this;
426 |       } else {
427 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
428 |       }
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     // arguments
432 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
433 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
434 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
435 |     }
436 | 
437 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
438 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
439 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
440 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
441 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
442 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
443 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
444 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
445 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
446 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
447 | 
448 |       // type from JSDocInfo
449 |       JSType parameterType = null;
450 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
451 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
452 | 
453 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
454 |         parameterType =
455 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
456 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
457 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
458 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
459 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
460 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
461 |       } else {
462 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
463 |       }
464 | 
465 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
466 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
467 |           isOptionalParam,
468 |           isVarArgs);
469 | 
470 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
471 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
476 |     if (!isVarArgs) {
477 |       while (oldParameterType != null && !isVarArgs) {
478 |         builder.newParameterFromNode(oldParameterType);
479 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
484 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
488 |     return this;
489 |   }

794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;

===== M0151 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if

     */
Method code:
1095 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1096 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1097 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1098 | 
1099 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1100 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1101 |           isGlobalVar &&
1102 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1103 |            parent.isFunction());
1104 | 
1105 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1106 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1107 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1108 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1109 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1110 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1111 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1112 | 
1113 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1114 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1115 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1116 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1117 |         }
1118 |       }
1119 | 
1120 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1121 |       // the extern info from the node.
1122 |       boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1123 |       Var newVar = null;
1124 | 
1125 |       // declared in closest scope?
1126 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1127 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1128 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1129 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1130 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1131 |       } else {
1132 |         if (!inferred) {
1133 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1134 |         }
1135 | 
1136 |         newVar =
1137 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1138 | 
1139 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1140 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1141 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1142 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1143 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1144 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1145 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1146 |           }
1147 |         }
1148 |       }
1149 | 
1150 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1151 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1152 |       if (fnType != null &&
1153 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1154 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1155 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1156 |             !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1157 |           // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1158 |           FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1159 |           ObjectType.Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1160 | 
1161 |           // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1162 |           // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1163 |           // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1164 |           // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1165 |           // the same node.
1166 |           //
1167 |           // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1168 |           // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1169 |           prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1170 | 
1171 |           String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1172 | 
1173 |           // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1174 |           // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1175 |           // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1176 |           Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1177 |           if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1178 |             scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1179 |           }
1180 | 
1181 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1182 |               n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1183 |               /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1184 |               superClassCtor == null ||
1185 |               superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1186 |                   getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1187 | 
1188 |           // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1189 |           // it constructs itself.
1190 |           if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1191 |               !isExtern &&
1192 |               // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1193 |               // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1194 |               // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1195 |               // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1196 |               // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1197 |               // this.
1198 |               variableName.equals(
1199 |                   fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1200 |             compiler.report(
1201 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1202 |                     fnType.isConstructor() ?
1203 |                     CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1204 |                     variableName));
1205 |           }
1206 |         }
1207 |       }
1208 | 
1209 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1210 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1211 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1212 |         if (inferred) {
1213 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1214 |               type == null ?
1215 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1216 |               type,
1217 |               n);
1218 |         } else {
1219 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1220 |         }
1221 |       }
1222 | 
1223 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1224 |           && type != null
1225 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1226 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1227 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1228 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1229 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1230 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1231 |         globalThisCtor
1232 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1233 |       }
1234 |     }

===== M0172 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 134-246
Documentation:

Method code:
134 |   @Override
135 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
136 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
137 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
138 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
139 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
140 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
141 |     // worthwhile.
142 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
143 |     Node condition = null;
144 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
145 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
146 | 
147 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
148 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
149 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
150 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
151 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
152 | 
153 |       switch (branch) {
154 |         case ON_TRUE:
155 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
156 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
157 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
158 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
159 | 
160 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
161 | 
162 |             if (item.isVar()) {
163 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
164 |             }
165 |             if (item.isName()) {
166 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
167 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
168 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
169 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
170 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
171 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
172 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
173 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
174 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
175 |                 }
176 |               }
177 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
178 |             }
179 |             newScope = informed;
180 |             break;
181 |           }
182 | 
183 |           // FALL THROUGH
184 | 
185 |         case ON_FALSE:
186 |           if (condition == null) {
187 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
188 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
189 |               condition = source;
190 | 
191 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
192 |               // of the loop.
193 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
194 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
195 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
196 |               }
197 |             }
198 |           }
199 | 
200 |           if (condition != null) {
201 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
202 |                 condition.isOr()) {
203 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
204 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
205 |               // scope on false.
206 |               //
207 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
208 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
209 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
210 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
211 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
212 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
213 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
214 | 
215 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
216 |               // of the loop.
217 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
218 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
219 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
220 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
221 |               }
222 |               newScope =
223 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
224 |                       condition,
225 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
226 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
227 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
228 |             } else {
229 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
230 |               // of the loop.
231 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
232 |                 conditionFlowScope =
233 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
234 |               }
235 |               newScope =
236 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
237 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
238 |             }
239 |           }
240 |           break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
244 |     }
245 |     return result;
246 |   }

/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.

===== M0187 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 258-308
Documentation:

Method code:
258 |   @Override
259 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
260 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
261 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
262 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
263 |         return true;
264 |       }
265 | 
266 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
267 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
268 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate through all
269 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
270 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
271 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
272 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
273 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
274 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
275 |         return false;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
279 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
280 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
281 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
282 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
283 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
284 |             return false;
285 |           }
286 |         }
287 | 
288 |         return true;
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
292 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
293 | 
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
299 |       }
300 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
301 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
302 |           return false;
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |       return true;
306 |     }
307 |     return false;
308 |   }

1028 |   private static void toStringTreeHelper(Node n, int level, Appendable sb)
1029 |       throws IOException {
1030 |     for (int i = 0; i != level; ++i) {
1031 |       sb.append("    ");
1032 |     }

1034 |     sb.append('\n');
1035 |     for (Node cursor = n.getFirstChild();
1036 |          cursor != null;
1037 |          cursor = cursor.getNext()) {
1038 |       toStringTreeHelper(cursor, level + 1, sb);
1039 |     }
1040 |   }

===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 120-285
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

===== M0199 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 841-950
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the JsDoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
841 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
842 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
843 |         @Nullable String name,
844 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
845 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
846 | 
847 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
848 | 
849 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
850 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
851 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
852 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
853 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
854 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
855 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
856 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
857 |             functionType = aliasedType;
858 | 
859 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
860 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
861 |             }
862 |           }
863 |         }
864 |       }
865 | 
866 |       if (functionType == null) {
867 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
868 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
869 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
870 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
871 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
872 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
873 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
874 | 
875 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
876 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
877 | 
878 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
879 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
880 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
881 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
882 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
883 |           }
884 |         }
885 | 
886 |         if (functionType == null) {
887 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
888 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
889 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
890 |           Var ownerVar = null;
891 |           String propName = null;
892 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
893 |           if (ownerName != null) {
894 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
895 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
896 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
897 |             }
898 |             if (name != null) {
899 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
900 |             }
901 |           }
902 | 
903 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
904 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
905 |             overriddenPropType =
906 |                 findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
907 |           }
908 | 
909 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
910 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
911 |                   scope)
912 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
913 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
914 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
915 |               .inferReturnType(info)
916 |               .inferInheritance(info);
917 | 
918 | 
919 |           // Infer the context type.
920 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
921 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
922 |             builder.inferThisType(
923 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
924 |             searchedForThisType = true;
925 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
926 |             // If 'this' has a type, use that instead.
927 |             // This is a hack, necessary because CollectProperties (below)
928 |             // doesn't run with the scope that it's building,
929 |             // so scope.getTypeOfThis() will be wrong.
930 |             JSType injectedThisType = ownerNode.getJSType();
931 |             builder.inferThisType(
932 |                 info,
933 |                 injectedThisType == null ?
934 |                 scope.getTypeOfThis() : injectedThisType);
935 |             searchedForThisType = true;
936 |           }
937 | 
938 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
939 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
940 |           }
941 | 
942 |           functionType = builder
943 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
944 |               .buildAndRegister();
945 |         }
946 |       }
947 | 
948 |       // all done
949 |       return functionType;
950 |     }

===== M0203 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 948-1007
Documentation:

Method code:
948 |   private void toString(
949 |       StringBuilder sb,
950 |       boolean printSource,
951 |       boolean printAnnotations,
952 |       boolean printType) {
953 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
954 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
955 |       sb.append(' ');
956 |       sb.append(getString());
957 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
958 |       sb.append(' ');
959 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
960 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
961 |       // toStringTree.
962 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
963 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
964 |       } else {
965 |         sb.append(first.getString());
966 |       }
967 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
968 |       sb.append(' ');
969 |       sb.append(getDouble());
970 |     }
971 |     if (printSource) {
972 |       int lineno = getLineno();
973 |       if (lineno != -1) {
974 |         sb.append(' ');
975 |         sb.append(lineno);
976 |       }
977 |     }
978 | 
979 |     if (printAnnotations) {
980 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
981 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
982 |         int type = keys[i];
983 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
984 |         sb.append(" [");
985 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
986 |         sb.append(": ");
987 |         String value;
988 |         switch (type) {
989 |           default:
990 |             value = x.toString();
991 |             break;
992 |         }
993 |         sb.append(value);
994 |         sb.append(']');
995 |       }
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     if (printType) {
999 |       if (jsType != null) {
1000 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1001 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1002 |           sb.append(" : ");
1003 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1004 |         }
1005 |       }
1006 |     }
1007 |   }

/**
   * Create a new union type, with an alternate that has already been
   * parsed. The alternate may be null.
   */
Method code:
2001 |   private Node parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken token, Node alternate) {
2002 |     Node union = newNode(Token.PIPE);
2003 |     if (alternate != null) {
2004 |       union.addChildToBack(alternate);
2005 |     }
2006 | 
2007 |     Node expr = null;
2008 |     do {
2009 |       if (expr != null) {
2010 |         skipEOLs();
2011 |         token = next();
2012 |         Preconditions.checkState(
2013 |             token == JsDocToken.PIPE || token == JsDocToken.COMMA);
2014 | 
2015 |         boolean isPipe = token == JsDocToken.PIPE;
2016 |         if (isPipe && match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
2017 |           // We support double pipes for backwards compatibility.
2018 |           next();
2019 |         }
2020 |         skipEOLs();
2021 |         token = next();
2022 |       }
2023 |       expr = parseTypeExpression(token);
2024 |       if (expr == null) {
2025 |         return null;
2026 |       }
2027 | 
2028 |       union.addChildToBack(expr);
2029 |       // We support commas for backwards compatibility.
2030 |     } while (match(JsDocToken.PIPE, JsDocToken.COMMA));
2031 | 
2032 |     if (alternate == null) {
2033 |       skipEOLs();
2034 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
2035 |         return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rp");
2036 |       }
2037 |       next();
2038 |     }
2039 |     return union;
2040 |   }

===== M0217 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 597-636
Documentation:

Method code:
597 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
598 |     String varName = n.getString();
599 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
600 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
601 |     if (value != null) {
602 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
603 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
604 |           getJSType(value));
605 |       return scope;
606 |     } else {
607 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
608 |       if (var != null) {
609 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
610 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
611 | 
612 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
613 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
614 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
615 |         boolean unflowable = isInferred &&
616 |             isUnflowable(syntacticScope.getVar(varName));
617 | 
618 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
619 |         // inferred variable.
620 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
621 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
622 |             isInferred &&
623 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
624 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
625 | 
626 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
627 |           type = var.getType();
628 |           if (type == null) {
629 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
630 |           }
631 |         }
632 |       }
633 |     }
634 |     n.setJSType(type);
635 |     return scope;
636 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)

Original range: 309-316
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
310 |     if (c <= 127) {
311 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
312 |     } else {
313 |       return c == 0xA0
314 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
315 |     }
316 |   }

===== M0228 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
1140 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1141 |     if (resolved) {
1142 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
1143 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
1144 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
1145 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1146 |       }
1147 |       return resolveResult;
1148 |     }
1149 |     resolved = true;
1150 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
1151 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
1152 |     return resolveResult;
1153 |   }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1076-1104
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1076 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1077 |     // unknown
1078 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1079 |       return true;
1080 |     }
1081 |     // equality
1082 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1083 |       return true;
1084 |     }
1085 |     // all type
1086 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1087 |       return true;
1088 |     }
1089 |     // unions
1090 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1091 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1092 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1093 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1094 |           return true;
1095 |         }
1096 |       }
1097 |     }
1098 |     // proxy types
1099 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1100 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1101 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1102 |     }
1103 |     return false;
1104 |   }

===== M0254 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1686-1727
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1686 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1687 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1688 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1689 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1690 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1691 |       // {?} - right curly
1692 |       // {?=} - equals
1693 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1694 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1695 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1696 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1697 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1698 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1699 |       token = next();
1700 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1701 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1702 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1703 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1704 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1705 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1706 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1707 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1708 |       }
1709 | 
1710 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1711 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1712 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1713 |     } else {
1714 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1715 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1716 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1717 |           next();
1718 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1719 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1720 |           next();
1721 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1722 |         }
1723 |       }
1724 | 
1725 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1726 |     }
1727 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java

392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

/**
   * Declares a refined type in {@code scope} for the name represented by
   * {@code node}. It must be possible to refine the type of the given node in
   * the given scope, as determined by {@link #getTypeIfRefinable}.
   */
Method code:
157 |   protected void declareNameInScope(FlowScope scope, Node node, JSType type) {
158 |     switch (node.getType()) {
159 |       case Token.NAME:
160 |         scope.inferSlotType(node.getString(), type);
161 |         break;
162 | 
163 |       case Token.GETPROP:
164 |         String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
165 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(qualifiedName);
166 | 
167 |         JSType origType = node.getJSType();
168 |         origType = origType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : origType;
169 |         scope.inferQualifiedSlot(node, qualifiedName, origType, type);
170 |         break;
171 | 
172 |         // "this" references aren't currently modeled in the CFG.
173 | 
174 |       default:
175 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("Node cannot be refined. \n" +
176 |             node.toStringTree());
177 |     }

===== M0299 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 796-834
Documentation:

Method code:
796 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
797 |       Node callNode) {
798 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
799 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
800 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
801 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
802 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
803 |       return scope;
804 |     }
805 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
806 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
807 |       return scope;
808 |     }
809 |     JSType assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType(
810 |         callNode, registry);
811 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
812 | 
813 |     JSType narrowed;
814 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
815 |     if (assertedType == null) {
816 |       // Handle arbitrary expressions within the assert.
817 |       scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
818 |           assertedNode, scope, true);
819 |       // Build the result of the assertExpression
820 |       narrowed = getJSType(assertedNode).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
821 |     } else {
822 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
823 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
824 |       narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(assertedType);
825 |       if (assertedNodeName != null && type.differsFrom(narrowed)) {
826 |         scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
827 |       }
828 |     }
829 | 
830 |     if (getJSType(callNode).differsFrom(narrowed)) {
831 |       callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
832 |     }
833 |     return scope;
834 |   }

===== M0302 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1197-1248
Documentation:

Method code:
1197 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1198 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1199 |     // We often have a couple of different types to choose from for the
1200 |     // property. Ordered by accuracy, we have
1201 |     // 1) A locally inferred qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1202 |     // 2) A globally declared qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1203 |     // 3) A property on the owner type (which is on objType)
1204 |     // 4) A name in the type registry (as a last resort)
1205 |     JSType unknownType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1206 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1207 |     boolean isLocallyInferred = false;
1208 | 
1209 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1210 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1211 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1212 |     if (var != null) {
1213 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1214 |       if (varType != null) {
1215 |         boolean isDeclared = !var.isTypeInferred();
1216 |         isLocallyInferred = (var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName));
1217 |         if (isDeclared || isLocallyInferred) {
1218 |           propertyType = varType;
1219 |         }
1220 |       }
1221 |     }
1222 | 
1223 |     if (propertyType == null && objType != null) {
1224 |       JSType foundType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1225 |       if (foundType != null) {
1226 |         propertyType = foundType;
1227 |       }
1228 |     }
1229 | 
1230 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType())
1231 |         && qualifiedName != null) {
1232 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1233 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1234 |       if (regType != null) {
1235 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1236 |       }
1237 |     }
1238 | 
1239 |     if (propertyType == null) {
1240 |       return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1241 |     } else if (propertyType.equals(unknownType) && isLocallyInferred) {
1242 |       // If the type has been checked in this scope,
1243 |       // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1244 |       return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1245 |     } else {
1246 |       return propertyType;
1247 |     }
1248 |   }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1766-1801
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1766 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1767 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1768 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1769 |     }
1770 | 
1771 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1772 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1773 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1774 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1775 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1776 |       skipEOLs();
1777 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1778 |         next();
1779 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1780 |       }
1781 |     }
1782 | 
1783 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1784 | 
1785 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1786 |       next();
1787 |       skipEOLs();
1788 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1789 |       if (memberType != null) {
1790 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1791 | 
1792 |         skipEOLs();
1793 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1794 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1795 |         }
1796 | 
1797 |         next();
1798 |       }
1799 |     }
1800 |     return typeNameNode;
1801 |   }
