===== M0326 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0341 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1491-1645
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1491 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1492 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1493 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1494 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1495 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1496 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1497 | 
1498 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1499 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1500 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1501 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1502 | 
1503 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1504 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1505 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1506 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1507 |         }
1508 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1509 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1510 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1511 | 
1512 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1513 |         return createOptionalType(
1514 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1515 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1516 | 
1517 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1518 |         return createOptionalType(
1519 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1520 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1521 | 
1522 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1523 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1524 | 
1525 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1526 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1527 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1528 | 
1529 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1530 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1531 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1532 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1533 |           builder.addAlternate(
1534 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1535 |         }
1536 |         return builder.build();
1537 | 
1538 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1539 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1540 | 
1541 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1542 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1543 | 
1544 |       case Token.STRING:
1545 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1546 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1547 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1548 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1549 |         }
1550 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1551 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1552 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1553 |           if (typeList != null &&
1554 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1555 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1556 |             JSType parameterType =
1557 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1558 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1559 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1560 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1561 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1562 |               JSType indexType =
1563 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1564 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1565 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1566 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1567 |             }
1568 |           }
1569 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1570 |         } else {
1571 |           return namedType;
1572 |         }
1573 | 
1574 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1575 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1576 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1577 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1578 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1579 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1580 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1581 |           thisType =
1582 |               ObjectType.cast(
1583 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1584 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1585 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1586 |           if (thisType == null) {
1587 |             reporter.warning(
1588 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1589 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1590 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1591 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1592 |                 sourceName,
1593 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1594 |           }
1595 | 
1596 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1597 |           current = current.getNext();
1598 |         }
1599 | 
1600 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1601 | 
1602 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1603 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1604 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1605 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1606 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1607 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1608 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1609 |               } else {
1610 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1611 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1612 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1613 |               }
1614 |             } else {
1615 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1616 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1617 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1618 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1619 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1620 |                   reporter.warning(
1621 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1622 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1623 |                 }
1624 |               } else {
1625 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1626 |               }
1627 |             }
1628 |           }
1629 |           current = current.getNext();
1630 |         }
1631 | 
1632 |         JSType returnType =
1633 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1634 | 
1635 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1636 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1637 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1638 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1639 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1640 |             .build();
1641 |     }
1642 | 
1643 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1644 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1645 |   }

===== M0354 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Scope.getVar(String)

Original range: 539-548
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns the variable, may be null
   */
Method code:
539 |   public Var getVar(String name) {
540 |     Var var = vars.get(name);
541 |     if (var != null) {
542 |       return var;
543 |     } else if (parent != null) { // Recurse up the parent Scope

===== M0377 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1733-1759
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1733 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1734 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1735 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1736 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1737 |       skipEOLs();
1738 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1739 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1740 |       skipEOLs();
1741 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1742 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1743 |       skipEOLs();
1744 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1745 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1746 |       String string = stream.getString();
1747 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1748 |         skipEOLs();
1749 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1750 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1751 |         return newStringNode(string);
1752 |       } else {
1753 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1754 |       }
1755 |     }
1756 | 
1757 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1758 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1759 |   }

===== M0382 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 243-301
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
243 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
244 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
245 |     if (oldType == null) {
246 |       return this;
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
250 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
251 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
252 |       // Not a function literal.
253 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
254 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
255 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
256 |       }
257 |     } else {
258 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
259 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
260 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
261 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
262 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
263 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
264 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
265 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
266 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
267 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
268 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
269 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
270 | 
271 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
272 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
273 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
274 | 
275 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
276 |           // arguments.
277 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
278 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
279 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
280 |           }
281 |         } else {
282 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
283 |               paramBuilder,
284 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
285 |               warnedAboutArgList,
286 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
287 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
288 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
289 |         }
290 |       }
291 | 
292 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal,
293 |       // but make them optional.
294 |       while (oldParams.hasNext()) {
295 |         paramBuilder.newOptionalParameterFromNode(oldParams.next());
296 |       }
297 | 
298 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
299 |     }
300 |     return this;
301 |   }

===== M0389 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 409-422
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
409 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
410 |       Node ret;
411 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
412 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
413 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
414 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
415 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
416 |       } else {
417 |         ret = transform(n);
418 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
419 |       }
420 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
421 |       return ret;
422 |     }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 692-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0395 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

37 |     public final L apply(List<L> values) {
38 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!values.isEmpty());
39 |       int size = values.size();
40 |       if (size == 1) {
41 |         return values.get(0);
42 |       } else if (size == 2) {

44 |       } else {

46 |         return apply(

48 |             apply(values.subList(mid, size)));
49 |       }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.checkForClassDefiningCalls(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1337-1409
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1337 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1338 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1339 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1340 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1341 |       if (relationship != null) {
1342 |         FunctionType superCtor = getFunctionType(
1343 |             scope.getVar(relationship.superclassName));
1344 |         FunctionType subCtor = getFunctionType(
1345 |             scope.getVar(relationship.subclassName));
1346 |         if (superCtor != null && superCtor.isConstructor() &&
1347 |             subCtor != null && subCtor.isConstructor()) {
1348 |           ObjectType superClass = superCtor.getInstanceType();
1349 |           ObjectType subClass = subCtor.getInstanceType();
1350 | 
1351 |           // superCtor and subCtor might be structural constructors
1352 |           // (like {function(new:Object)}) so we need to resolve them back
1353 |           // to the original ctor objects.
1354 |           superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1355 |           subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1356 | 
1357 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS &&
1358 |               !superClass.isEmptyType() && !subClass.isEmptyType()) {
1359 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1360 |           }
1361 | 
1362 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1363 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1364 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1365 |           }
1366 |         }
1367 |       }
1368 | 
1369 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1370 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1371 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1372 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1373 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1374 |         if (objectType != null) {
1375 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1376 | 
1377 |           if (functionType != null) {
1378 |             FunctionType getterType =
1379 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1380 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1381 |                 objectType);
1382 |           }
1383 |         }
1384 |       }
1385 | 
1386 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1387 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1388 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1389 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1390 |       }
1391 | 
1392 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1393 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(n);
1394 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1395 |         if (objectLiteralCast.diagnosticType == null) {
1396 |           ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1397 |               typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1398 |           if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1399 |             setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1400 |           } else {
1401 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1402 |                     CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
1403 |           }
1404 |         } else {
1405 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1406 |                   objectLiteralCast.diagnosticType));
1407 |         }
1408 |       }
1409 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 248-411

Method code:
248 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
249 |     switch (n.getType()) {
250 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
251 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
252 |         break;
253 | 
254 |       case Token.NAME:
255 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
256 |         break;
257 | 
258 |       case Token.GETPROP:
259 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
260 |         break;
261 | 
262 |       case Token.AND:
263 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
264 |             .createChildFlowScope();
265 |         break;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.OR:
268 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
269 |             .createChildFlowScope();
270 |         break;
271 | 
272 |       case Token.HOOK:
273 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
274 |         break;
275 | 
276 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
277 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
278 |         break;
279 | 
280 |       case Token.CALL:
281 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
282 |         break;
283 | 
284 |       case Token.NEW:
285 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
286 |         break;
287 | 
288 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
289 |       case Token.ADD:
290 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
291 |         break;
292 | 
293 |       case Token.POS:
294 |       case Token.NEG:
295 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
296 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
297 |         break;
298 | 
299 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
300 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
301 |         break;
302 | 
303 |       case Token.THIS:
304 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
305 |         break;
306 | 
307 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
308 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
309 |       case Token.LSH:
310 |       case Token.RSH:
311 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
312 |       case Token.URSH:
313 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
314 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
315 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
316 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
317 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
318 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
319 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
320 |       case Token.DIV:
321 |       case Token.MOD:
322 |       case Token.BITAND:
323 |       case Token.BITXOR:
324 |       case Token.BITOR:
325 |       case Token.MUL:
326 |       case Token.SUB:
327 |       case Token.DEC:
328 |       case Token.INC:
329 |       case Token.BITNOT:
330 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
331 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
332 |         break;
333 | 
334 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
335 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
336 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
337 |         break;
338 | 
339 |       case Token.COMMA:
340 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
341 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
342 |         break;
343 | 
344 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
345 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
346 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
347 |         break;
348 | 
349 |       case Token.DELPROP:
350 |       case Token.LT:
351 |       case Token.LE:
352 |       case Token.GT:
353 |       case Token.GE:
354 |       case Token.NOT:
355 |       case Token.EQ:
356 |       case Token.NE:
357 |       case Token.SHEQ:
358 |       case Token.SHNE:
359 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
360 |       case Token.IN:
361 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
362 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
363 |         break;
364 | 
365 |       case Token.GETELEM:
366 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
367 |         break;
368 | 
369 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
370 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
371 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isGetProp()) {
372 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
373 |         }
374 |         break;
375 | 
376 |       case Token.SWITCH:
377 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
378 |         break;
379 | 
380 |       case Token.RETURN:
381 |         scope = traverseReturn(n, scope);
382 |         break;
383 | 
384 |       case Token.VAR:
385 |       case Token.THROW:
386 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
387 |         break;
388 | 
389 |       case Token.CATCH:
390 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
391 |         break;
392 |     }
393 |     if (!n.isFunction()) {
394 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
395 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
396 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
397 | 
398 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
399 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
400 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
401 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
402 |             n.getParent().isExprResult()) {
403 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
404 |         }
405 | 
406 |         n.setJSType(castType);
407 |       }
408 |     }
409 | 
410 |     return scope;
411 |   }

===== M0431 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.isConnectedInDirection(N,Predicate<E>,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 203-215
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
210 |         return true;
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:
913 |       case Token.GETPROP:
914 |       case Token.GETELEM:
915 |       case Token.THROW:
916 |       case Token.NEW:
917 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
918 |       case Token.INC:
919 |       case Token.DEC:
920 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
921 |         return true;
922 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
923 |         return false;
924 |     }
925 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
926 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
927 |         return true;
928 |       }
929 |     }
930 |     return false;
931 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a parameter type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @param lineno The line of the type expression.
   * @param startCharno The starting character position of the type expression.
   * @param matchingLC Whether the type expression starts with a "{".
   * @param onlyParseSimpleNames If true, only simple type names are parsed
   *     (via a call to parseTypeNameAnnotation instead of
   *     parseTypeExpressionAnnotation).
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1144 |   private Node parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken token, int lineno,
1145 |                                       int startCharno,
1146 |                                       boolean matchingLC,
1147 |                                       boolean onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1148 |     Node typeNode = null;
1149 | 
1150 |     if (onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1151 |       typeNode = parseTypeNameAnnotation(token);
1152 |     } else {
1153 |       typeNode = parseTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1154 |     }
1155 | 
1156 |     if (typeNode != null && !matchingLC) {
1157 |       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
1158 |     }
1159 | 
1160 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1161 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1162 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1163 | 
1164 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(
1165 |           typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endLineno, endCharno, matchingLC);
1166 |     }
1167 | 
1168 |     return typeNode;
1169 |   }

===== M0463 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 462-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
462 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
463 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
464 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
465 |     switch (left.getType()) {
466 |       case Token.NAME:
467 |         String varName = left.getString();
468 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
469 | 
470 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
471 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
472 |         // This has two purposes:
473 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
474 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
475 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
476 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
477 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
478 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
479 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
480 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
481 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
482 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
483 |         }
484 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
485 |             resultType : null);
486 | 
487 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
488 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
489 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
490 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
491 |         }
492 |         break;
493 |       case Token.GETPROP:
494 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
495 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
496 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
497 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
498 |               resultType);
499 |         }
500 | 
501 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
502 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
503 |         break;
504 |     }
505 |   }

/** Handle bleeding functions and function parameters. */
Method code:
1890 |     private void handleFunctionInputs(Node fnNode) {
1891 |       // Handle bleeding functions.
1892 |       Node fnNameNode = fnNode.getFirstChild();
1893 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
1894 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty()) {
1895 |         Scope.Var fnVar = scope.getVar(fnName);
1896 |         if (fnVar == null ||
1897 |             // Make sure we're not touching a native function. Native
1898 |             // functions aren't bleeding, but may not have a declaration
1899 |             // node.
1900 |             (fnVar.getNameNode() != null &&
1901 |                 // Make sure that the function is actually bleeding by checking
1902 |                 // if has already been declared.
1903 |                 fnVar.getInitialValue() != fnNode)) {
1904 |           defineSlot(fnNameNode, fnNode, fnNode.getJSType(), false);
1905 |         }
1906 |       }
1907 | 
1908 |       declareArguments(fnNode);

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 319-366
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
319 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
320 |     if (info != null) {
321 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
322 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
323 | 
324 |       // base type
325 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
326 |         if (isConstructor) {
327 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
328 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
329 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
330 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
331 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
332 |           }
333 |         } else {
334 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
335 |         }
336 |       }
337 | 
338 |       // implemented interfaces
339 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
340 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
341 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
342 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
343 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
344 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
345 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
346 |           }
347 |         }
348 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
349 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
350 |       }
351 | 
352 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
353 |       if (isInterface) {
354 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
355 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
356 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
357 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
358 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
359 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
360 |           }
361 |         }
362 |       }
363 |     }
364 | 
365 |     return this;
366 |   }

===== M0516 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1637-1653
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1637 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1638 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1639 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1640 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1641 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1642 |         next();
1643 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1644 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1645 |           next();
1646 |         }
1647 |         skipEOLs();
1648 |         token = next();
1649 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1650 |       }
1651 |     }
1652 |     return typeExpr;
1653 |   }

===== M0519 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 504-524
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
504 |   @Override
505 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
506 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
507 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
508 |     if (unknown) {
509 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
510 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
511 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
512 |         unknown = false;
513 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
514 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
515 |             unknown = true;
516 |             break;
517 |           }
518 |         }
519 |       } else {
520 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |     return unknown;
524 |   }

68 |       this.lastSlot = null;
69 |       this.depth = 0;
70 |       this.parent = cache.linkedEquivalent;
71 |     } else {

73 |       this.depth = directParent.depth + 1;
74 |       this.parent = directParent;
75 |     }
76 |   }

===== M0530 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 345-371
Documentation:

Method code:
345 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
346 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
347 |     // two cases:
348 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
349 |     // and
350 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
351 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
352 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
353 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
354 |     //
355 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
356 |     //
357 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
358 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
359 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
360 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
361 |     //
362 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
363 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
364 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
365 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
366 | 
367 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
368 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
369 |     }
370 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
371 |   }

===== M0532 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1662-1692
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub declarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1662 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1663 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1664 |         Node n = stub.node;
1665 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1666 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1667 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1668 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1669 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1670 | 
1671 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1672 |           continue;
1673 |         }
1674 | 
1675 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1676 |         // in the type registry.
1677 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1678 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1679 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1680 | 
1681 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1682 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1683 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1684 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1685 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1686 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1687 |         } else {
1688 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1689 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1690 |         }
1691 |       }
1692 |     }

===== M0536 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1567-1582
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1567 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1568 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1569 |       String name = getString();
1570 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1571 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1572 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1573 |       if (left == null) {
1574 |         return null;
1575 |       }
1576 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1577 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1578 |       return "this";
1579 |     } else {
1580 |       return null;
1581 |     }
1582 |   }

===== M0538 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 619-631
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
619 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
620 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
621 |     if (typeSet == null) {
622 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
623 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
627 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
628 | 
629 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
630 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
631 |   }

===== M0561 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 246-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
246 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
247 |     this.options = options;
248 |     if (errorManager == null) {
249 |       if (outStream == null) {
250 |         setErrorManager(
251 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
252 |       } else {
253 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
254 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
255 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
256 |         setErrorManager(printer);
257 |       }
258 |     }
259 | 
260 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
261 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
262 |       options.checkTypes = true;
263 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
264 |       options.checkTypes = false;
265 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
266 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
267 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
268 |       // parser type warnings.
269 |       options.setWarningLevel(
270 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
271 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
272 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
276 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
277 |       options.setWarningLevel(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
279 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
283 |       options.setWarningLevel(
284 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
285 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
289 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
290 |     guards.add(
291 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
292 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
293 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
294 | 
295 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
296 | 
297 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
298 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
299 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
300 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
301 |     // error off.
302 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
303 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
304 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
305 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
306 |     }
307 | 
308 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
309 |   }

===== M0570 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1968-2008
Documentation:

Method code:
1968 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1969 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
1970 |         return;
1971 |       }
1972 | 
1973 |       if (n.isReturn() && n.getFirstChild() != null) {
1974 |         data.get(t.getScopeRoot()).recordNonEmptyReturn();
1975 |       }
1976 | 
1977 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() <= 2) {
1978 |         // We only need to worry about escaped variables at depth 3.
1979 |         // An variable escaped at depth 2 is, by definition, a global variable.
1980 |         // We treat all global variables as escaped by default, so there's
1981 |         // no reason to do this extra computation for them.
1982 |         return;
1983 |       }
1984 | 
1985 |       if (n.isName() && NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
1986 |         String name = n.getString();
1987 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
1988 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
1989 |         if (var != null) {
1990 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
1991 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
1992 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordEscapedVarName(name);
1993 |           }
1994 |         }
1995 |       } else if (n.isGetProp() && n.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
1996 |           NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
1997 |         String name = NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(n).getString();
1998 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
1999 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
2000 |         if (var != null) {
2001 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
2002 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2003 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode())
2004 |                 .recordEscapedQualifiedName(n.getQualifiedName());
2005 |           }
2006 |         }
2007 |       }
2008 |     }

===== M0602 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 151-197
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
151 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
152 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
153 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.BLOCK:
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |       case Token.TRY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
184 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
185 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
186 |         } else {
187 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
188 |         }
189 |       case Token.SWITCH:
190 |       case Token.CASE:
191 |       case Token.CATCH:
192 |       case Token.WITH:
193 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |       default:
195 |         return false;
196 |     }
197 |   }

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;

Original range: 312-334
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the {@code prototype} property of this function type. This is
   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.
   */
Method code:
312 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {
313 |     // lazy initialization of the prototype field
314 |     if (prototypeSlot == null) {
315 |       String refName = getReferenceName();
316 |       if (refName == null) {
317 |         // Someone is trying to access the prototype of a structural function.
318 |         // We don't want to give real properties to this prototype, because
319 |         // then it would propagate to all structural functions.
320 |         setPrototypeNoCheck(
321 |            registry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE),
322 |            null);
323 |       } else {
324 |         setPrototype(
325 |             new PrototypeObjectType(
326 |                 registry,
327 |                 this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype",
328 |                 registry.getNativeObjectType(OBJECT_TYPE),
329 |                 isNativeObjectType()),
330 |             null);
331 |       }
332 |     }
333 |     return (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
334 |   }

===== M0622 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.ClosureReverseAbstractInterpreter.ClosureReverseAbstractInterpreter(CodingConvention,JSTypeRegistry)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/ClosureReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 118-203
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a {@link ClosureReverseAbstractInterpreter}.
   */
Method code:
118 |   public ClosureReverseAbstractInterpreter(CodingConvention convention,
119 |       final JSTypeRegistry typeRegistry) {
120 |     super(convention, typeRegistry);
121 |     this.restricters =
122 |       new ImmutableMap.Builder<String, Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>>()
123 |       .put("isDef", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
124 |         @Override
125 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
126 |           if (p.outcome) {
127 |             return getRestrictedWithoutUndefined(p.type);
128 |           } else {
129 |             return null;
130 |           }
131 |          }
132 |       })
133 |       .put("isNull", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
134 |         @Override
135 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
136 |           if (p.outcome) {
137 |             return getNativeType(NULL_TYPE);
138 |           } else {
139 |             return getRestrictedWithoutNull(p.type);
140 |           }
141 |         }
142 |       })
143 |       .put("isDefAndNotNull", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
144 |         @Override
145 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
146 |           if (p.outcome) {
147 |             return getRestrictedWithoutUndefined(
148 |                 getRestrictedWithoutNull(p.type));
149 |           } else {
150 |             return null;
151 |           }
152 |         }
153 |       })
154 |       .put("isString", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
155 |         @Override
156 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
157 |           return getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(p.type, "string", p.outcome);
158 |         }
159 |       })
160 |       .put("isBoolean", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
161 |         @Override
162 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
163 |           return getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(p.type, "boolean", p.outcome);
164 |         }
165 |       })
166 |       .put("isNumber", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
167 |         @Override
168 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
169 |           return getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(p.type, "number", p.outcome);
170 |         }
171 |       })
172 |       .put("isFunction", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
173 |         @Override
174 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
175 |           return getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(p.type, "function", p.outcome);
176 |         }
177 |       })
178 |       .put("isArray", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
179 |         @Override
180 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
181 |           if (p.type == null) {
182 |             return p.outcome ? getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE) : null;
183 |           }
184 | 
185 |           Visitor<JSType> visitor = p.outcome ? restrictToArrayVisitor :
186 |               restrictToNotArrayVisitor;
187 |           return p.type.visit(visitor);
188 |         }
189 |       })
190 |       .put("isObject", new Function<TypeRestriction, JSType>() {
191 |         @Override
192 |         public JSType apply(TypeRestriction p) {
193 |           if (p.type == null) {
194 |             return p.outcome ? getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE) : null;
195 |           }

===== M0643 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2347-2363
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2347 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2348 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2349 |     int c;
2350 |     while (true) {
2351 |       c = stream.getChar();
2352 |       if (c == ' ') {
2353 |         continue;
2354 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2355 |         matchedLc = true;
2356 |         break;
2357 |       } else {
2358 |         break;
2359 |       }
2360 |     }
2361 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2362 |     return matchedLc;
2363 |   }

===== M0671 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0679 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 94-153
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
94 |   @Override
95 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
96 |     SubclassRelationship relationship =
97 |         super.getClassesDefinedByCall(callNode);
98 |     if (relationship != null) return relationship;
99 | 
100 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
101 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
102 |     if (type != null) {
103 |       Node subclass = null;
104 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
105 | 
106 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
107 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
108 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
109 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
110 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
111 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
112 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
113 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
114 |           callName.isGetProp();
115 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
116 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
117 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
118 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
119 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
120 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
121 |       } else {
122 |         return null;
123 |       }
124 | 
125 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
126 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
127 |         // inheritance.
128 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
129 |           return null;
130 |         }
131 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
132 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
133 |             return null;
134 |           }
135 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
136 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
137 |         }
138 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
139 |       }
140 | 
141 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
142 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
143 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
144 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
145 |       if (subclass != null &&
146 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
147 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
148 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
149 |       }
150 |     }
151 | 
152 |     return null;
153 |   }
