/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0694 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 191-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
191 |   @Override
192 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
193 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
194 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
195 |     // show up in the type registry.
196 |     Scope newScope = null;
197 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
198 |     if (parent == null) {
199 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
200 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
201 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
202 | 
203 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
204 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
205 | 
206 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
207 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
208 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
209 |     } else {
210 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
211 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
212 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
213 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
217 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
218 | 
219 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
220 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
221 |     // build properties on.
222 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
223 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
224 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
225 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
226 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
227 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
228 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
229 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
230 |         }
231 |       }
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     if (parent == null) {
235 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
236 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
237 |           delegateCallingConventions);
238 |     }
239 |     return newScope;
240 |   }

===== M0706 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

===== M0711 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1051-1053
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1051 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1052 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1053 |   }

Method code:
176 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
177 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
178 |                                    String sourceString,
179 |                                    Config config,
180 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
181 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,
182 |         config, errorReporter);
183 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
184 | 
185 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
186 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
187 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
188 |             !irFactory.parsedComments.contains(comment)) {
189 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment, irNode);
190 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK_COMMENT) {
191 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 | 
196 |     irFactory.setFileOverviewJsDoc(irNode);
197 | 
198 |     return irNode;
199 |   }

===== M0723 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3040-3059
Documentation:
/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
3040 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
3041 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
3042 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = isFunctionDeclaration(n);
3043 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
3044 |       return n.getFirstChild();
3045 |     } else if (parent.isName()) {
3046 |       return parent;
3047 |     } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3048 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
3049 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3050 |       return parent;
3051 |     } else if (
3052 |         (parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3053 |         parent.isOr() ||
3054 |         parent.isAnd() ||
3055 |         (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3056 |       return getBestLValue(parent);
3057 |     }
3058 |     return null;
3059 |   }

===== M0734 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 182-951
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
182 |   boolean parse() {
183 |     int lineno;
184 |     int charno;
185 | 
186 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
187 |     JSTypeExpression type;
188 | 
189 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
190 |     skipEOLs();
191 | 
192 |     JsDocToken token = next();
193 | 
194 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
195 | 
196 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
197 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
198 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
199 |       token = blockInfo.token;
200 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
201 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
202 |       }
203 |     } else {
204 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
205 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
206 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
207 |         // what it was.
208 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
213 |     retry: for (;;) {
214 |       switch (token) {
215 |         case ANNOTATION:
216 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
217 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
218 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
219 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
220 | 
221 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
222 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
223 |             if (annotation == null) {
224 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
225 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |             } else {
227 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
228 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
229 | 
230 |               switch (annotation) {
231 |                 case AUTHOR:
232 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
233 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
234 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
235 | 
236 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
237 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
238 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                     } else {
240 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
241 |                     }
242 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
243 |                   } else {
244 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
245 |                   }
246 |                   continue retry;
247 | 
248 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
249 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
250 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
251 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case CONSTANT:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
258 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
259 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
260 |                   }
261 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
262 |                   continue retry;
263 | 
264 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
265 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
266 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
267 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
268 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
269 |                     } else {
270 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
271 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
272 |                     }
273 |                   }
274 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
275 |                   continue retry;
276 | 
277 |                 case DEPRECATED:
278 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
279 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
280 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
281 |                   }
282 | 
283 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
284 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
285 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
286 | 
287 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
288 | 
289 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
290 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
291 |                   }
292 | 
293 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
294 |                   continue retry;
295 | 
296 |                 case INTERFACE:
297 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
298 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
299 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
300 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
301 |                     } else {
302 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
303 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
304 |                     }
305 |                   }
306 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
307 |                   continue retry;
308 | 
309 |                 case DESC:
310 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
311 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
312 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
313 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
314 |                     continue retry;
315 |                   } else {
316 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
317 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
318 | 
319 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
320 | 
321 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
322 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
323 |                     continue retry;
324 |                   }
325 | 
326 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
327 |                   String fileOverview = "";
328 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
329 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
330 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
331 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
332 | 
333 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
334 | 
335 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
336 |                   } else {
337 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
338 |                   }
339 | 
340 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview)) {
341 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
342 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
343 |                   }
344 |                   continue retry;
345 | 
346 |                 case LICENSE:
347 |                 case PRESERVE:
348 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
349 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
350 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
351 | 
352 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
353 | 
354 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
355 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
356 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
357 |                     }
358 |                   }
359 | 
360 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
361 |                   continue retry;
362 | 
363 |                 case ENUM:
364 |                   token = next();
365 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
366 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
367 | 
368 |                   type = null;
369 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
370 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
371 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
372 |                   }

375 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
376 |                   }
377 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
378 |                     parser.addTypeWarning(
379 |                         "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
380 |                   }

382 |                   continue retry;
383 | 
384 |                 case EXPORT:
385 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
386 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
387 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
388 |                   }
389 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
390 |                   continue retry;
391 | 
392 |                 case EXPOSE:
393 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExpose()) {
394 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.expose",
395 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
396 |                   }
397 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
398 |                   continue retry;
399 | 
400 |                 case EXTERNS:
401 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
402 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
403 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
404 |                   }
405 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
406 |                   continue retry;
407 | 
408 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
409 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
410 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
411 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
412 |                   }
413 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
414 |                   continue retry;
415 | 
416 |                 case EXTENDS:
417 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
418 |                   skipEOLs();
419 |                   token = next();
420 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
421 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
422 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
423 | 
424 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
425 |                     token = next();
426 |                     matchingRc = true;
427 |                   }
428 | 
429 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
430 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
431 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
432 | 
433 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
434 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
435 | 
436 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
437 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
438 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
439 |                     }
440 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
441 | 
442 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
443 |                       // record the extended type, check later
444 |                       extendedTypes.add(new ExtendedTypeInfo(
445 |                           type, stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno()));
446 |                     } else {
447 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
448 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
449 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
450 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
451 |                             lineno, charno);
452 |                       }
453 |                     }
454 |                     token = next();
455 |                     if (matchingRc) {
456 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
457 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
458 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
459 |                       }
460 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
461 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
462 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
463 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
464 |                     }
465 |                   } else {
466 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
467 |                   }
468 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
469 |                   continue retry;
470 | 
471 |                 case HIDDEN:
472 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
473 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
474 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
475 |                   }
476 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
477 |                   continue retry;
478 | 
479 |                 case LENDS:
480 |                   skipEOLs();
481 | 
482 |                   matchingRc = false;
483 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
484 |                     token = next();
485 |                     matchingRc = true;
486 |                   }
487 | 
488 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
489 |                     token = next();
490 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordLends(stream.getString())) {
491 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.incompatible",
492 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
493 |                     }
494 |                   } else {
495 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.missing",
496 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
497 |                   }
498 | 
499 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
500 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
501 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
502 |                   }
503 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
504 |                   continue retry;
505 | 
506 |                 case MEANING:
507 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
508 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
509 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
510 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
511 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
512 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
513 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
514 |                   }
515 |                   continue retry;
516 | 
517 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
518 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
519 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
520 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
521 |                   }
522 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
523 |                   continue retry;
524 | 
525 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
526 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
527 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
528 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
529 |                   }
530 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
531 |                   continue retry;
532 | 
533 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
534 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
535 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
536 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
537 |                   }
538 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
539 |                   continue retry;
540 | 
541 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
542 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
543 |                   continue retry;
544 | 
545 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
546 |                 case OVERRIDE:
547 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
548 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
549 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
550 |                   }
551 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
552 |                   continue retry;
553 | 
554 |                 case THROWS:
555 |                   skipEOLs();
556 |                   token = next();
557 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
558 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
559 |                   type = null;
560 | 
561 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
562 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
563 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
564 | 
565 |                     if (type == null) {
566 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
567 |                       // recovering parsing
568 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
569 |                       continue retry;
570 |                     }
571 |                   }
572 | 
573 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
574 |                   token = current();
575 | 
576 |                   // Save the throw type.
577 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
578 | 
579 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
580 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
581 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
582 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
583 | 
584 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
585 | 
586 |                     if (description.length() > 0) {
587 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
588 |                     }
589 | 
590 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
591 |                   } else {
592 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
593 |                   }
594 |                   continue retry;
595 | 
596 |                 case PARAM:
597 |                   skipEOLs();
598 |                   token = next();
599 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
600 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
601 |                   type = null;
602 | 
603 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
604 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
605 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
606 | 
607 |                     if (type == null) {
608 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
609 |                       // recovering parsing
610 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
611 |                       continue retry;
612 |                     }
613 |                     skipEOLs();
614 |                     token = next();
615 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
616 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
617 |                   }
618 | 
619 |                   String name = null;
620 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
621 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
622 |                     token = next();
623 |                   }
624 | 
625 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
626 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
627 |                         lineno, charno);
628 |                   } else {
629 |                     name = stream.getString();
630 | 
631 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
632 |                       token = next();
633 | 
634 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter
635 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
636 |                       // system.
637 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
638 |                         token = next();
639 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
640 |                           token = next();
641 |                         }
642 |                       }
643 | 
644 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
645 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
646 |                       } else if (type != null) {
647 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
648 |                         // already.
649 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
650 |                       }
651 |                     }
652 | 
653 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
654 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
655 |                     // for handling properties of params.
656 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
657 |                       name = null;
658 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
659 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
660 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
661 |                             lineno, charno);
662 |                       } else {
663 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
664 |                             lineno, charno);
665 |                       }
666 |                     }
667 |                   }
668 | 
669 |                   if (name == null) {
670 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
671 |                     continue retry;
672 |                   }
673 | 
674 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, sourceFile, lineno, charno);
675 | 
676 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
677 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
678 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
679 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
680 | 
681 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
682 | 
683 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
684 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
685 |                           paramDescription);
686 |                     }
687 | 
688 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
689 |                   } else {
690 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
691 |                   }
692 |                   continue retry;
693 | 
694 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
695 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
696 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
697 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
698 |                   }
699 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
700 |                   continue retry;
701 | 
702 |                 case PRIVATE:
703 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
704 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
705 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
706 |                   }
707 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
708 |                   continue retry;
709 | 
710 |                 case PROTECTED:
711 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
712 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
713 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
714 |                   }
715 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
716 |                   continue retry;
717 | 
718 |                 case PUBLIC:
719 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
720 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
721 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
722 |                   }
723 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
724 |                   continue retry;
725 | 
726 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
727 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
728 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
729 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
730 |                   }
731 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
732 |                   continue retry;
733 | 
734 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
735 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
736 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
737 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
738 |                   }
739 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
740 |                   continue retry;
741 | 
742 |                 case MODIFIES:
743 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
744 |                   continue retry;
745 | 
746 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
747 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
748 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
749 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
750 |                   }
751 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
752 |                   continue retry;
753 | 
754 |                 case SEE:
755 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
756 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
757 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
758 | 
759 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
760 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
761 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
762 |                     } else {
763 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
764 |                     }
765 | 
766 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
767 |                   } else {
768 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
769 |                   }
770 |                   continue retry;
771 | 
772 |                 case SUPPRESS:
773 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
774 |                   continue retry;
775 | 
776 |                 case TEMPLATE:
777 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
778 |                   List<String> names = Lists.newArrayList(
779 |                       Splitter.on(',')
780 |                           .trimResults()
781 |                           .split(templateInfo.string));
782 | 
783 |                   if (names.size() == 0 || names.get(0).length() == 0) {
784 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
785 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
786 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeNames(names)) {
787 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
788 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
789 |                   }
790 | 
791 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
792 |                   continue retry;
793 | 
794 |                 case IDGENERATOR:
795 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordIdGenerator()) {
796 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.idgen",
797 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
798 |                   }
799 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
800 |                   continue retry;
801 | 
802 |                 case VERSION:
803 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
804 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
805 | 
806 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
807 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
808 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
809 |                   } else {
810 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
811 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
812 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
813 |                     }
814 |                   }
815 | 
816 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
817 |                   continue retry;
818 | 
819 |                 case DEFINE:
820 |                 case RETURN:
821 |                 case THIS:
822 |                 case TYPE:
823 |                 case TYPEDEF:
824 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
825 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
826 | 
827 |                   Node typeNode = null;
828 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
829 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
830 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
831 |                     // it as the unknown type.
832 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
833 |                   } else {
834 |                     skipEOLs();
835 |                     token = next();
836 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
837 |                   }
838 | 
839 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
840 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
841 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
842 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
843 |                     }
844 |                   }
845 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
846 | 
847 |                   if (type == null) {
848 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
849 |                     // recovering parsing
850 |                   } else {
851 |                     switch (annotation) {
852 |                       case DEFINE:
853 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
854 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
855 |                               lineno, charno);
856 |                         }
857 |                         break;
858 | 
859 |                       case RETURN:
860 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
861 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
862 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
863 |                           break;
864 |                         }
865 | 
866 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
867 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
868 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
869 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
870 | 
871 |                           String returnDescription =
872 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
873 | 
874 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
875 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
876 |                                 returnDescription);
877 |                           }
878 | 
879 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
880 |                         } else {
881 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
882 |                         }
883 |                         continue retry;
884 | 
885 |                       case THIS:
886 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
887 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
888 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
889 |                         }
890 |                         break;
891 | 
892 |                       case TYPE:
893 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
894 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
895 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
896 |                         }
897 |                         break;
898 | 
899 |                       case TYPEDEF:
900 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
901 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
902 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
903 |                         }
904 |                         break;
905 |                     }
906 |                   }
907 | 
908 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
909 |                   continue retry;
910 |               }
911 |             }
912 |           }
913 |           break;
914 | 
915 |         case EOC:
916 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
917 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
918 |           }
919 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
920 |           return true;
921 | 
922 |         case EOF:
923 |           // discard any accumulated information
924 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
925 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
926 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
927 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
928 |           return false;
929 | 
930 |         case EOL:
931 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
932 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
933 |           }
934 |           token = next();
935 |           continue retry;
936 | 
937 |         default:
938 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
939 |             token = next();
940 |             continue retry;
941 |           } else {
942 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
943 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
944 |             continue retry;
945 |           }
946 |       }
947 | 
948 |       // next token
949 |       token = next();
950 |     }
951 |   }

557 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
558 |       switch (n.getType()) {
559 |         case Token.NULL:
560 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
561 |           break;
562 | 
563 |         case Token.VOID:
564 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
565 |           break;
566 | 
567 |         case Token.STRING:
568 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
569 |           break;
570 | 
571 |         case Token.NUMBER:
572 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
573 |           break;
574 | 
575 |         case Token.TRUE:
576 |         case Token.FALSE:
577 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
578 |           break;
579 | 
580 |         case Token.REGEXP:
581 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
582 |           break;

586 |           if (info != null &&
587 |               info.getLendsName() != null) {
588 |             if (lentObjectLiterals == null) {
589 |               lentObjectLiterals = Lists.newArrayList();
590 |             }
591 |             lentObjectLiterals.add(n);
592 |           } else {
593 |             defineObjectLiteral(n);
594 |           }
595 |           break;
596 |

598 |           // we will need to put ARRAYLIT here as well.
599 |       }
600 |     }

===== M0752 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

506 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
507 |           break;

511 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);

516 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);

539 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
540 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&

543 |           }

548 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&

552 |         }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(Node)

/**
   * Gets the condition of an ON_TRUE / ON_FALSE CFG edge.
   * @param n a node with an outgoing conditional CFG edge
   * @return the condition node or null if the condition is not obviously a node
   */
Method code:
1679 |   static Node getConditionExpression(Node n) {
1680 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1681 |       case Token.IF:
1682 |       case Token.WHILE:
1683 |         return n.getFirstChild();
1684 |       case Token.DO:

1696 |     }
1697 |     throw new IllegalArgumentException(n + " does not have a condition.");

   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.

Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;

379 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {

/**
     * Defines a typed variable. The defining node will be annotated with the
     * variable's type of {@link JSTypeNative#UNKNOWN_TYPE} if its type is
     * inferred.
     *
     * Slots may be any variable or any qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     */
Method code:
1064 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1065 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(inferred || type != null);
1066 | 
1067 |       // Only allow declarations of NAMEs and qualified names.
1068 |       // Object literal keys will have to compute their names themselves.
1069 |       if (n.isName()) {
1070 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
1071 |             parent.isFunction() ||
1072 |             parent.isVar() ||
1073 |             parent.isParamList() ||
1074 |             parent.isCatch());
1075 |       } else {
1076 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
1077 |             n.isGetProp() &&
1078 |             (parent.isAssign() ||
1079 |              parent.isExprResult()));
1080 |       }
1081 |       defineSlot(n, parent, n.getQualifiedName(), type, inferred);
1082 |     }

===== M0806 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1910-1912
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1910 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1911 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1912 |   }

===== M0811 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

===== M0812 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

===== M0814 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

/**

   * The iterator does not support the optional operation
   * {@link Iterator#remove()}.</p>
